49   + @pytest.mark.windows
50   + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
51   +     "pattern",
52   +     [
53   +         "grating",
54   +         "polar_grating",
55   +         "checker",
56   +         "dots_grid",
57   +         "dots_rand",
58   +         "noise_binary",
59   +         "noise_gray",
60   +         "filtered_noise",
61   +         "calibration_grid",
62   +         "flat",
63   +     ],
64   + )
65   + def test_native_physical_field_shader_matches_mm_sampler(pattern):
66   +     import numpy as np
67   +
68   +     moderngl = pytest.importorskip("moderngl")
69   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.field_shader import layer_fragment
70   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.shaders import _LAYER_VERTEX
71   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.physical_field import raster_field
72   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import TextureRecipe
73   +
74   +     params = (
75   +         {"orientation_deg": 37.0, "spatial_period_mm": 10.0, "feature_sharpness": 0.3}
76   +         if pattern == "grating"
77   +         else {}
78   +     )
79   +     if pattern in {"noise_binary", "noise_gray"}:
80   +         params = {"element_size_mm": 3.0, "blur_mm": 0.4, "seed": 192}
81   +     if pattern == "filtered_noise":
82   +         params = {"seed": 534, "black_fraction": 0.3, "edge_smooth_mm": 1.2}
83   +     if pattern == "dots_rand":
84   +         params = {"seed": 2**64 - 59, "blur_mm": 0.4}
85   +     recipe = TextureRecipe(pattern, params, 512, None, None, True)
86   +     context = moderngl.create_standalone_context(require=430)
87   +     owned = []
88   +
89   +     def track(value):
90   +         owned.append(value)
91   +         return value
92   +
93   +     try:
94   +         program = track(
95   +             context.program(
96   +                 vertex_shader=_LAYER_VERTEX, fragment_shader=layer_fragment(recipe)
97   +             )
98   +         )
99   +         buffer = track(context.buffer(struct.pack("8f", 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1)))
100  +         array = track(context.vertex_array(program, [(buffer, "2f", "in_uv")]))
101  +         flags = track(context.buffer(bytes(32)))
102  +         flags.bind_to_storage_buffer(3)
103  +         program["mode"].value = 9
104  +         program["mean_rgb"].value = (0.5,) * 3
105  +         program["modulation_rgb"].value = (0.5,) * 3
106  +         program["opacity"].value = program["contrast"].value = 1.0
107  +         program["clip_vertices"].write(struct.pack("8f", -1, -1, 1, -1, -1, 1, 1, 1))
108  +         for size, extent in (
109  +             ((160, 96), (40, 24)),
110  +             ((200, 80), (40, 24)),
111  +             ((80, 160), (40, 24)),
112  +             ((120, 100), (60, 40)),
113  +         ):
114  +             target = track(context.texture(size, 4, dtype="f4"))
115  +             fbo = track(context.framebuffer([target]))
116  +             fbo.use()
117  +             context.viewport = (0, 0, *size)
118  +             if "field_extent_mm" in program:
119  +                 program["field_extent_mm"].value = extent
120  +             if "field_phase_mm" in program:
121  +                 program["field_phase_mm"].value = (0.137, -0.219)
122  +             from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_draw import draw_layer
123  +
124  +             values = dict(
125  +                 width=extent[0],
126  +                 height=extent[1],
127  +                 period_x=2.0,
128  +                 period_y=3.0,
129  +                 phase_x=0.137 / 2,
130  +                 phase_y=-0.219 / 3,
131  +             )
132  +             setting = NS(
133  +                 kind="texture",
134  +                 space=NS(kind="physical_surface"),
135  +                 pattern=NS(kind="image_tile", asset_id="field"),
136  +                 mean_linear_rgb=(0.5,) * 3,
137  +                 modulation_linear_rgb=(0.5,) * 3,
138  +             )
139  +             layouts = tuple(
140  +                 NS(instance_id="field", output_id="out", name=name, binding_id=name)
141  +                 for name in ("clip_vertices_map", "field_extent_mm", "field_phase_mm")
142  +             )
143  +             artifact = NS(
144  +                 identity=NS(resource_generation="generation"),
145  +                 display=NS(active_outputs=(NS(output_id="out"),)),
146  +                 uniform_layouts=layouts,
147  +             )
148  +             frame = NS(
149  +                 quad_array=array,
150  +                 layer_program=program,
151  +                 field_programs={"field": (program, array)},
152  +             )
153  +             evidence = draw_layer(
154  +                 moderngl,
155  +                 frame,
156  +                 artifact,
157  +                 {},
158  +                 "out",
159  +                 "map",
160  +                 InstanceSnapshot(
161  +                     "field", "texture", True, tuple(values.items()), setting
162  +                 ),
163  +                 [(-1, -1), (1, -1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)],
164  +             )
165  +             assert {item.binding_id for item in evidence} == {
166  +                 item.binding_id for item in layouts
167  +             }
168  +
169  +             actual = np.frombuffer(target.read(), dtype=np.float32).reshape(
170  +                 size[1], size[0], 4
171  +             )[::-1, :, :3]
172  +             expected = (
173  +                 raster_field(pattern, params, *extent, *size, offset_mm=(0.137, -0.219))
174  +                 / 255
175  +             )
176  +             np.testing.assert_allclose(actual, expected, atol=1.1 / 255, rtol=0)
177  +     finally:
178  +         for item in reversed(owned):
179  +             item.release()
180  +         context.release()
181  +

255  +     )

258  +         update={"source": source, "epochs": epochs, "uniform_layouts": layouts}
259  +     )

261  +         "grating",

263  +         512,

268  +     image = ImagePixels(
269  +         1,

348  +         np.testing.assert_allclose(actual, expected, atol=1.1 / 255, rtol=0)
349  +     finally:
350  +         renderer.release()
351  +         assert (
352  +             not renderer._outputs and not renderer._resource_builder.partial_allocations
353  +         )
354  +         context.release()
355  +

357  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("bits", [8, 10])
358  + def test_projector_window_recreation_preserves_operator_cursor(bits):
359  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import configure_window
360  +
361  +     output = (
362  +         make_prepared_trial()
363  +         .display.active_outputs[0]
364  +         .model_copy(update={"rgb_bits_per_channel": bits})
365  +     )
366  +
367  +     class Windows:
368  +         TRUE, FALSE = 1, 0
369  +         hints = {}
370  +         cursor = (300, 200)
371  +
372  +         def __getattr__(self, name):
373  +             if name.isupper():
374  +                 return name
375  +             raise AttributeError(name)
376  +
377  +         def default_window_hints(self):
378  +             self.hints = {self.CENTER_CURSOR: self.TRUE, self.AUTO_ICONIFY: self.TRUE}
379  +
380  +         def window_hint(self, hint, value):
381  +             self.hints[hint] = value
382  +
383  +         def create_fullscreen(self):
384  +             if self.hints[self.CENTER_CURSOR]:
385  +                 self.cursor = (2560, 360)
386  +
387  +     windows = Windows()
388  +     for _ in range(3):
389  +         configure_window(windows, output)
390  +         windows.create_fullscreen()
391  +         assert windows.cursor == (300, 200)
392  +         assert windows.hints[windows.AUTO_ICONIFY] == windows.FALSE
393  +         assert windows.hints[windows.RED_BITS] == bits
394  +         assert windows.hints[windows.GREEN_BITS] == bits
395  +         assert windows.hints[windows.BLUE_BITS] == bits
396  +         assert windows.hints[windows.REFRESH_RATE] > 0

425  + def test_reference_bars_share_exact_pixel_spans_and_restore_scissor():
426  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.calibration_bars import (
427  +         reference_lengths,
428  +         reference_scale,
429  +     )
430  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.reference_bars import draw_reference_bars
431  +
432  +     class Context:
433  +         scissor = (1, 2, 3, 4)
434  +
435  +         def clear(self, *color, alpha, viewport):
436  +             calls.append((self.scissor, color, alpha, viewport))
437  +
438  +     calls = []
439  +     context = Context()
440  +     width, height = 1279, 719
441  +     x, y = reference_lengths(width, height)
442  +     draw_reference_bars(context, width, height)
443  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
444  +     assert calls[-2][0][2] == x
445  +     assert calls[-1][0][3] == y
446  +     assert [rectangle for rectangle, color, _, _ in calls if color == (1, 1, 1)] == [
447  +         (0, 0, width, 2),
448  +         (0, height - 2, width, 2),
449  +         (0, 0, 2, height),
450  +         (width - 2, 0, 2, height),
451  +     ]
452  +     assert all(scissor == viewport for scissor, _, _, viewport in calls)
453  +     scale = reference_scale(width, height, x / 2, y / 2, 1.5)
454  +     assert scale.mm_per_pixel_x == scale.mm_per_pixel_y == 0.5
455  +     assert scale.projector_distance_mm == pytest.approx(width * 0.5 * 1.5)
456  +     calls.clear()
457  +     draw_reference_bars(context, width, height, x_px=200, y_px=100)
458  +     assert calls[-2][0][2] == 200 and calls[-1][0][3] == 100
459  +     assert len(calls) == 8
460  +     direct = reference_scale(width, height, 80, 60, x_px=200, y_px=100)
461  +     assert direct.projector_distance_mm is None
462  +     assert direct.projected_width_mm == pytest.approx(width * 0.4)
463  +     custom = reference_scale(width, height, 80, 60, 1.5, x_px=200, y_px=100)
464  +     assert custom.mm_per_pixel_x == pytest.approx(0.4)
465  +     assert custom.mm_per_pixel_y == pytest.approx(0.6)
466  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
467  +     for length in (0, -1, 1.5, width + 1, float("nan"), float("inf")):
468  +         with pytest.raises(ValueError):
469  +             reference_lengths(width, height, length, 100)
470  +     for invalid in (0, -1, float("nan"), float("inf")):
471  +         with pytest.raises(ValueError):
472  +             reference_scale(width, height, invalid, y / 2, 1.5)
473  +     context.clear = lambda *args, **kwargs: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
474  +         RuntimeError("render failure")
475  +     )
476  +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="render failure"):
477  +         draw_reference_bars(context, width, height)
478  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
479  +

481  + def test_output_blackouts_restore_scissor_on_success_and_failure():
482  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.display_profile import PixelRect
483  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.output_masks import clear_regions
484  +
485  +     context = NS(scissor=(9, 9, 2, 2))
486  +     calls = []
487  +
488  +     def clear(*color, alpha, viewport):
489  +         calls.append((context.scissor, color, alpha, viewport))
490  +
491  +     zone = PixelRect(x=1, y=2, width=3, height=4)
492  +     clear_regions(context, clear, (zone,))
493  +     assert calls == [((1, 2, 3, 4), (0, 0, 0), 1, (1, 2, 3, 4))]
494  +     assert context.scissor == (9, 9, 2, 2)
495  +
496  +     def fail(*args, **kwargs):
497  +         raise RuntimeError("clear failed")
498  +
499  +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="clear failed"):
500  +         clear_regions(context, fail, (zone,))
501  +     assert context.scissor == (9, 9, 2, 2)

504  + def test_idle_uses_prepared_valid_area_union_and_restores_scissor():
505  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.artifact_models import GeometricProfile
506  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.output_masks import clear_valid_background
507  +
508  +     display = make_prepared_trial().display
509  +     output = display.active_outputs[0]
510  +     mappings = [m for m in display.active_mappings if m.output_id == output.output_id]
511  +     profiles = {}
512  +     for mapping in mappings:
513  +         profiles[mapping.mapping_id] = GeometricProfile.model_validate(
514  +             dict(
515  +                 format_version=1,
516  +                 calibration_id="valid-area",
517  +                 mapping_id=mapping.mapping_id,
518  +                 surface_id=mapping.surface_id,
519  +                 output_id=output.output_id,
520  +                 output_width=output.width_px,
521  +                 output_height=output.height_px,
522  +                 viewport=mapping.viewport,
523  +                 rows=2,
524  +                 columns=2,
525  +                 vertices=tuple(
526  +                     dict(uv=p, xy=p) for p in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1))
527  +                 ),
528  +                 orientation="preserving",
529  +                 diagonal="bottom_left_to_top_right",
530  +                 mask=None,
531  +                 weight=None,
532  +                 overlap_group=None,
533  +                 intended_coverage=((0, 0), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1)),
534  +                 coverage_tolerance=1e-6,
535  +                 triangle_area_tolerance=1e-9,
536  +                 valid_area=(0.2, 0.3, 0.8, 0.9),
537  +             )
538  +         )
539  +     calls = []
540  +     context = NS(scissor=(2, 3, 4, 5))
541  +     context.clear = lambda *color, **kwargs: calls.append(
542  +         (context.scissor, color, kwargs)
543  +     )
544  +     clear_valid_background(
545  +         context, (0.3, 0.4, 0.5), output, display, NS(content=profiles)
546  +     )
547  +     assert calls[0][0] is None and calls[0][1] == (0, 0, 0)
548  +     assert len(calls) == len(mappings) + 1
549  +     assert all(c[0] == (20, 30, 60, 60) and c[1] == (0.3, 0.4, 0.5) for c in calls[1:])
550  +     assert context.scissor == (2, 3, 4, 5)
551  +     for key in profiles:
552  +         profiles[key] = profiles[key].model_copy(update={"valid_area": None})
553  +     calls.clear()
554  +     clear_valid_background(
555  +         context, (0.3, 0.4, 0.5), output, display, NS(content=profiles)
556  +     )
557  +     assert len(calls) == 1 and calls[0][1] == (0.3, 0.4, 0.5)

560  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("per_output", [False, True])
561  + def test_photodiode_pulse_renders_after_blackouts_and_black_frame(per_output):
562  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.display_profile import (
563  +         PhotodiodePatch,
564  +         PixelRect,
565  +     )
566  +
567  +     artifact = make_prepared_trial()
568  +     output = artifact.display.active_outputs[0]
569  +     zone = PixelRect(x=0, y=0, width=10, height=10)
570  +     patch = PhotodiodePatch(
571  +         rect=PixelRect(x=12, y=13, width=8, height=6),
572  +         background_rect=PixelRect(x=5, y=6, width=30, height=40),
573  +         high_linear_rgb=(1, 1, 1),
574  +         low_linear_rgb=(0, 0, 0),
575  +     )
576  +     display = artifact.display.model_copy(
577  +         update={
578  +             "outputs": (output.model_copy(update={"blackout_zones": (zone,)}),),
579  +             "photodiode_patch": patch,
580  +             "photodiode_output_id": output.output_id,
581  +         }
582  +     )
583  +     if per_output:
584  +         display = display.model_copy(
585  +             update={
586  +                 "photodiode_output_id": None,
587  +                 "photodiode_patch": None,
588  +                 "photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch},
589  +             }
590  +         )
591  +     artifact = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
592  +     calls = []
593  +     context = NS(scissor=None, screen=None, disable=lambda *_: None)
594  +     program = {
595  +         key: NS(value=None)
596  +         for key in (
597  +             "source_tex",
598  +             "lut_tex",
599  +             "calibrated",
600  +             "lut_size",
601  +             "marker_active",
602  +             "marker_rect",
603  +             "marker_rgb",
604  +         )
605  +     }
606  +     frame = NS(
607  +         context=context,
608  +         output_fbo=NS(clear=lambda *c, **kw: calls.append((c, kw["viewport"]))),
609  +         device_fbo=NS(use=lambda: None),
610  +         output_texture=NS(size=(100, 100), use=lambda *_: None),
611  +         lut_texture=NS(size=(2, 1), use=lambda *_: None),
612  +         output_program=program,
613  +         output_quad_array=NS(render=lambda **_: calls.append("marker/output")),
614  +     )
615  +     renderer = ModernGLSceneRenderer()
616  +     renderer._moderngl = NS(BLEND=1, TRIANGLE_STRIP=5)
617  +     renderer._apply_output(frame, artifact, output.output_id, True, 0)
618  +     assert calls == [
619  +         ((0, 0, 0), (0, 0, 10, 10)),
620  +         ((0, 0, 0), (5, 6, 30, 40)),
621  +         "marker/output",
622  +     ]
623  +     assert program["marker_rect"].value == (0.12, 0.13, 0.2, 0.19)
624  +     assert program["marker_rgb"].value == (1, 1, 1)
625  +     assert context.scissor is None
626  +     if per_output:
627  +         other = output.model_copy(
628  +             update={
629  +                 "output_id": "other",
630  +                 "device_identity": "other",
631  +                 "blackout_zones": (),
632  +             }
633  +         )
634  +         extra = patch.model_copy(
635  +             update={
636  +                 "rect": PixelRect(x=20, y=21, width=2, height=3),
637  +                 "background_rect": None,
638  +             }
639  +         )
640  +         display = display.model_copy(
641  +             update={
642  +                 "outputs": (output, other),
643  +                 "photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch, "other": extra},
644  +             }
645  +         )
646  +         second = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
647  +         calls.clear()
648  +         renderer._apply_output(frame, second, "other", False, 0)
649  +         assert calls == ["marker/output"]
650  +         assert program["marker_rect"].value == (0.2, 0.21, 0.22, 0.24)
651  +         assert program["marker_rgb"].value == (0, 0, 0)
652  +         display = display.model_copy(
653  +             update={"photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch}}
654  +         )
655  +         renderer._apply_output(
656  +             frame, artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display}), "other", True, 0
657  +         )
658  +         assert program["marker_active"].value == 0
659  +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fit inside"):
660  +         PhotodiodePatch.model_validate(
661  +             dict(patch.model_dump(), rect=dict(x=0, y=0, width=1, height=1))
662  +         )
663  +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="exceeds"):
664  +         type(output).model_validate(
665  +             dict(
666  +                 output.model_dump(),
667  +                 blackout_zones=(dict(x=99, y=0, width=2, height=2),),
668  +             )
669  +         )

672  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("matches", [0, 2])
673  + def test_saved_monitor_requires_exact_unique_active_identity(monkeypatch, matches):
674  +     from cephvr.platform.windows import display_identity
675  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort
676  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import DisplayUnavailableError
677  +
678  +     monkeypatch.setattr(display_identity, "monitor_interface", lambda *_: "saved")
679  +     glfw = NS(
680  +         get_monitors=lambda: list(range(matches)),
681  +         get_win32_adapter=lambda monitor: monitor,
682  +         get_win32_monitor=lambda monitor: monitor,
683  +     )
684  +     message = (
685  +         "Windows does not detect the assigned display"
686  +         if matches == 0
687  +         else f"matched {matches} active"
688  +     )
689  +     with pytest.raises(DisplayUnavailableError, match=message):
690  +         ModernGLPort._resolve_monitor("SAVED", glfw)
691  +     glfw.get_monitors = lambda: [17]
692  +     assert ModernGLPort._resolve_monitor("SAVED", glfw) == 17

695  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("mapped", [False, True])
696  + def test_missing_second_monitor_opens_no_windows_and_releases_glfw(tmp_path, mapped):
697  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort
698  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import DisplayUnavailableError
699  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.types import ResourceReleaseReport
700  +
701  +     display = make_prepared_trial().display
702  +     first = display.active_outputs[0]
703  +     second = first.model_copy(
704  +         update={"output_id": "second", "device_identity": "missing"}
705  +     )
706  +     display = display.model_copy(update={"outputs": (first, second)})
707  +     if mapped:
708  +         display = display.model_copy(
709  +             update={
710  +                 "mappings": tuple(
711  +                     mapping.model_copy(update={"output_id": second.output_id})
712  +                     for mapping in display.mappings
713  +                 )
714  +             }
715  +         )
716  +     resolved, terminated = [], []
717  +
718  +     def resolve(identity, glfw):
719  +         resolved.append(identity)
720  +         if identity == "missing":
721  +             raise DisplayUnavailableError("missing saved projector")
722  +         return object()
723  +
724  +     port = ModernGLPort(tmp_path, announce=lambda *_: None, monitor_resolver=resolve)
725  +     # Any window or GL call would fail: identity preflight must precede creation.
726  +     port._glfw = NS(terminate=lambda: terminated.append(True))
727  +     port._moderngl = object()
728  +     with pytest.raises(DisplayUnavailableError, match="missing saved") as failure:
729  +         port.initialize_display(display)
730  +     faces = (
731  +         ", ".join(mapping.surface_id.capitalize() for mapping in display.mappings)
732  +         if mapped
733  +         else "second"
734  +     )
735  +     assert f"{faces} projector: missing saved projector" in str(failure.value)
736  +     assert "Saved display: missing (output second)" in str(failure.value)
737  +     assert "Refresh in Devices > Projectors" in failure.value.remedy
738  +     assert resolved == [first.device_identity, "missing"]
739  +     assert terminated == [True]
740  +     assert not port._outputs and not port._window_obligations
741  +     assert port.release() == ResourceReleaseReport(("visual_stimulus:glfw",))
742  +

744  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("cleanup_fails", [False, True])
745  + def test_wrong_adapter_rejection_requires_native_context_and_window_release(
746  +     monkeypatch, tmp_path, cleanup_fails
747  + ):
748  +     from cephvr.platform.windows import nvidia_device
749  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering import native
750  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import (
751  +         DisplayGpuUnavailableError,
752  +         NativeRenderingError,
753  +     )
754  +
755  +     selected = nvidia_device.NvidiaDevice(0, "GPU-board", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti")
756  +     monkeypatch.setattr(nvidia_device, "sys", NS(platform="win32"))
757  +     monkeypatch.setattr(nvidia_device, "_enumerate_cuda_devices", lambda: [selected])
758  +     released, destroyed, terminated = [], [], []
759  +
760  +     def release():
761  +         released.append(True)
762  +         if cleanup_fails and len(released) == 1:
763  +             raise RuntimeError("context release unconfirmed")
764  +
765  +     context = NS(
766  +         info={"GL_VENDOR": "ATI Technologies Inc.", "GL_RENDERER": "AMD Radeon"},
767  +         screen=object(),
768  +         release=release,
769  +     )
770  +     monkeypatch.setattr(native, "attach_window_context", lambda _: context)
771  +     monkeypatch.setattr(native, "configure_window", lambda *_: None)
772  +     monkeypatch.setattr(native, "hide_output_shell_entry", lambda *_: None)
773  +     port = native.ModernGLPort(
774  +         tmp_path, announce=lambda *_: None, monitor_resolver=lambda *_: object()
775  +     )
776  +     port._glfw = NS(
777  +         create_window=lambda *_: object(),
778  +         get_current_context=lambda: None,
779  +         make_context_current=lambda *_: None,
780  +         destroy_window=destroyed.append,
781  +         terminate=lambda: terminated.append(True),
782  +     )
783  +     port._moderngl = object()
784  +     expected = NativeRenderingError if cleanup_fails else DisplayGpuUnavailableError
785  +     with pytest.raises(expected, match="AMD Radeon") as failure:
786  +         port.initialize_display(make_prepared_trial().display)
787  +     assert isinstance(failure.value, DisplayGpuUnavailableError) is not cleanup_fails
788  +     assert bool(port._outputs) is cleanup_fails
789  +     if cleanup_fails:
790  +         assert not destroyed and not terminated
791  +         assert not port.release().outstanding
792  +     assert destroyed and terminated and not port._outputs
793  +

795  + def test_linear_rate_integral_and_keyframe_evaluation() -> None:
796  +     ramp = NS(kind="ramp", initial=0.0, slope_per_s=0.5)
797  +     assert integrate_function(ramp, 0, 2_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(1.0)

799  +         kind="keyframes",
800  +         interpolation="linear",
801  +         knots=(
802  +             NS(time=NS(seconds="0", ns=lambda: 0), value=0.0),
803  +             NS(time=NS(seconds="2", ns=lambda: 2_000_000_000), value=4.0),
804  +         ),
805  +     )
806  +     assert evaluate_function(keyframes, 1_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(2.0)
807  +     assert integrate_function(keyframes, 0, 2_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(4.0)

810  + def test_instance_absence_pauses_motion_and_return_reanchors() -> None:
811  +     rate = NS(kind="rate", function=NS(kind="constant", value=2.0))
812  +     settings = NS(
813  +         instance_id="bar",
814  +         kind="image",

817  +         opacity=NS(kind="constant", value=1.0),

822  +     live.advance(1_000_000_000)

828  +         settings, action="resume", epoch_start_ns=5_000_000_000, now_ns=5_000_000_000
829  +     )
830  +     live.advance(6_000_000_000)
831  +     assert live.values["x"].value == pytest.approx(4.0)

834  + def test_off_axis_projection_and_float_color_boundaries() -> None:
835  +     frustum = off_axis_frustum(
836  +         observer=(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
837  +         bottom_left=(-1.0, -1.0, -2.0),
838  +         bottom_right=(1.0, -1.0, -2.0),
839  +         top_left=(-1.0, 1.0, -2.0),
840  +         near=0.1,
841  +         far=10.0,
842  +     )
843  +     assert frustum == pytest.approx((-0.05, 0.05, -0.05, 0.05))
844  +     assert source_over((0.5, 0.0, 0.0, 0.5), (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.0)) == pytest.approx(
845  +         (0.5, 0.0, 0.5, 1.0)
846  +     )
847  +     assert source_over((0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5), (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0)) == pytest.approx(
848  +         (0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 1.0)
849  +     )
850  +     assert interpolate_inverse_lut((0.0, 0.25, 1.0), 0.5) == pytest.approx(0.25)
851  +     assert quantize_device_code(1.1, 8) == (255, True)
852  +

854  + def test_projected_layer_vertices_are_retained_as_exact_shader_words() -> None:
855  +     corners = ((-1.0, -0.5), (0.5, -0.5), (0.5, 1.0), (-1.0, 1.0))
856  +     components = clip_vertex_components(corners)
857  +     assert components == (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, -0.5, -1.0, 1.0, 0.5, 1.0)
858  +     assert float32_words(components) == tuple(
859  +         int.from_bytes(struct.pack("<f", value), "little") for value in components
860  +     )

863  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["landmarks", "every_frame"])
864  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("pulse_enabled", [True, False])
865  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("multiple", [False, True])
866  + def test_engine_freezes_exact_provider_evidence_for_recording(
867  +     pulse_enabled, multiple, mode
868  + ) -> None:
869  +     from dataclasses import replace
870  +
871  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.types import ImageSnapshot
872  +
873  +     artifact = make_prepared_trial()
874  +     if not pulse_enabled:
875  +         artifact = artifact.model_copy(
876  +             update={
877  +                 "display": artifact.display.model_copy(
878  +                     update={
879  +                         "photodiode_enabled": False,
880  +                         "pacing_output_id": artifact.display.photodiode_output_id,
881  +                         "photodiode_output_id": "projector/disconnected",
882  +                     }
883  +                 )
884  +             }
885  +         )
886  +     artifact = artifact.model_copy(
887  +         update={
888  +             "display": artifact.display.model_copy(update={"photodiode_mode": mode})
889  +         }
890  +     )
891  +     display = artifact.display
892  +     output = display.outputs[0]
893  +     if multiple:
894  +         outputs = (
895  +             output,
896  +             output.model_copy(
897  +                 update={"output_id": "second", "device_identity": "second"}
898  +             ),
899  +             output.model_copy(
900  +                 update={"output_id": "blank", "device_identity": "blank"}
901  +             ),
902  +         )
903  +         display = display.model_copy(
904  +             update={
905  +                 "outputs": outputs,
906  +                 "photodiode_output_id": None,
907  +                 "photodiode_patch": None,
908  +                 "photodiode_patches": {
909  +                     o.output_id: artifact.display.photodiode_patch for o in outputs[:2]
910  +                 },
911  +                 "pacing_output_id": output.output_id,
912  +             }
913  +         )
914  +         artifact = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
915  +     outputs = display.active_outputs
916  +
917  +     class Port:
918  +         def initialize_display(self, selected):
919  +             return DisplayInitialization(
920  +                 tuple(o.output_id for o in outputs),
921  +                 tuple((o.output_id, 8, 8, 8) for o in outputs),
922  +                 tuple((o.output_id, o.width_px, o.height_px) for o in outputs),
923  +                 tuple(
924  +                     (o.output_id, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id))
925  +                     for o in outputs
926  +                 ),
927  +                 tuple(
928  +                     OutputActivity(
929  +                         o.output_id, 1, 2, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
930  +                     )
931  +                     for o in outputs
932  +                 ),
933  +             )
934  +
935  +         def prepare_trial(self, prepared, resources):
936  +             assert prepared == artifact
937  +             assert resources is not None
938  +
939  +         def select_images(self, trial_id, epoch_index, snapshots):
940  +             image = ImageSnapshot(
941  +                 snapshots[0].instance_id,
942  +                 "next",
943  +                 1,
944  +                 "previous",
945  +                 0,
946  +                 "residency_hold",
947  +                 True,
948  +             )
949  +             return (replace(snapshots[0], image_selection=image), *snapshots[1:])
950  +
951  +         def render(self, scene, state):
952  +             return RenderPassResult(
953  +                 tuple(
954  +                     RenderedOutput(o.output_id, o.width_px, o.height_px, 8, object())
955  +                     for o in outputs
956  +                 ),
957  +                 (),
958  +                 (),
959  +                 (),
960  +             )
961  +
962  +         def present(self, frames):
963  +             return tuple(
964  +                 OutputActivity(
965  +                     o.output_id, 10, 11, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
966  +                 )
967  +                 for o in outputs
968  +             )
969  +
970  +         def show_idle(self, selected):
971  +             return tuple(
972  +                 OutputActivity(
973  +                     o.output_id, 20, 21, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
974  +                 )
975  +                 for o in outputs
976  +             )
977  +
978  +     engine = RendererEngine(
979  +         Port(), clock_ns=lambda: 0, resource_provider=lambda _: object()
980  +     )
981  +     engine.initialize_display(display)
982  +     engine.prepare_trial(artifact)
983  +     cancelled_idle = engine.stop_trial()
984  +     assert {o.output_id for o in cancelled_idle} == {o.output_id for o in outputs}
985  +     engine.begin_trial(artifact.identity.trial_id, 0)
986  +     update = engine.render_tick(0)
987  +
988  +     assert update.evidence_state.scene_id == update.group.scene_id
989  +     assert update.evidence_state.evaluation_host_ns == 0
990  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].image_index == 0
991  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].requested_image_index == 1
992  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].repeated
993  +     assert update.evidence_state.active_instance_ids
994  +     assert len(update.evidence_submissions) == len(outputs)
995  +     for submission in update.evidence_submissions:
996  +         assert submission.phase == "returned"
997  +         expected_marker = submission.output_id in display.marker_patches
998  +         assert submission.marker_high == (
999  +             update.group.photodiode_high if expected_marker else None
1000 +         )
1001 +         assert submission.marker_index == (0 if expected_marker else None)
1002 +     if not pulse_enabled:
1003 +         assert update.group.photodiode_high is None
1004 +     next_update = engine.render_tick(1)
1005 +     if pulse_enabled:
1006 +         assert next_update.group.photodiode_high == (mode == "landmarks")
1007 +     stopped_idle = engine.stop_trial(2)
1008 +     assert {o.output_id for o in stopped_idle} == {o.output_id for o in outputs}
1009 +

1028 + def test_arena_model_and_physical_projection_are_finite_and_pose_sensitive():
1029 +     settings = NS(
1030 +         fixed_height_mm=100.0,
1031 +         fixed_pitch_deg=0.0,
1032 +         fixed_roll_deg=0.0,
1033 +         asset_to_world=(
1034 +             (1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1035 +             (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1036 +             (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
1037 +             (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
1038 +         ),
1039 +     )
1040 +     origin = arena_model_matrix(settings, {"x": 0.0, "y": 0.0, "yaw": 0.0})
1041 +     moved = arena_model_matrix(settings, {"x": 25.0, "y": -5.0, "yaw": 30.0})
1042 +     assert origin[0][3] == 0.0 and origin[1][3] == 0.0
1043 +     assert moved[0][3] == 25.0 and moved[1][3] == -5.0
1044 +     assert moved[0][0] == pytest.approx(3**0.5 / 2)
1045 +     assert len(gl_matrix_words(moved)) == 16

1047 +     display = make_prepared_trial().display

1050 +     assert len(projection) == 4 and all(len(row) == 4 for row in projection)
1051 +     assert all(value == pytest.approx(value) for row in projection for value in row)

1053 +
1054 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1055 +     "angles", [(0, 0, 0), (90, 0, 0), (35, -20, 17), (-175, 80, -110)]
1056 + )
1057 + def test_effective_arena_pose_matches_rendered_rigid_transform(angles):
1058 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.arena import effective_arena_pose
1059 +
1060 +     yaw, pitch, roll = angles
1061 +     settings = NS(
1062 +         kind="arena",
1063 +         instance_id="arena",
1064 +         world_frame_id="world",
1065 +         fixed_height_mm=12.0,
1066 +         fixed_pitch_deg=pitch,
1067 +         fixed_roll_deg=roll,
1068 +         asset_to_world=((1, 0, 0, 8), (0, 1, 0, 9), (0, 0, 1, 10), (0, 0, 0, 1)),
1069 +     )
1070 +     state = {"x": 2.0, "y": -3.0, "yaw": yaw}
1071 +     snapshot = InstanceSnapshot("arena", "arena", True, tuple(state.items()), settings)
1072 +     pose = effective_arena_pose(snapshot)
1073 +     assert pose.instance_id == "arena" and pose.frame_id == "world"
1074 +     assert pose.position_mm == (2.0, -3.0, 12.0)
1075 +     x, y, z, w = pose.orientation_xyzw
1076 +     assert x * x + y * y + z * z + w * w == pytest.approx(1)
1077 +     rotation = (
1078 +         (1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - z * w), 2 * (x * z + y * w)),
1079 +         (2 * (x * y + z * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - x * w)),
1080 +         (2 * (x * z - y * w), 2 * (y * z + x * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y)),
1081 +     )
1082 +     model = arena_model_matrix(settings, state)
1083 +     for row in range(3):
1084 +         assert model[row][:3] == pytest.approx(rotation[row])
1085 +         assert model[row][3] == pytest.approx(
1086 +             pose.position_mm[row]
1087 +             + sum(rotation[row][j] * settings.asset_to_world[j][3] for j in range(3))
1088 +         )

1134 + def test_gpu_diagnostic_ring_preserves_group_order_across_slot_reuse(monkeypatch):
1135 +     import sys
1136 +     from types import SimpleNamespace
1137 +
1138 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.diagnostics import DiagnosticRing
1139 +
1140 +     class Buffer:
1141 +         def __init__(self):
1142 +             self.glo = id(self)
1143 +
1144 +         def write(self, data):
1145 +             assert len(data) == 16
1146 +
1147 +         def read(self, size):
1148 +             assert size == 16
1149 +             return bytes(16)
1150 +
1151 +     class Context:
1152 +         def buffer(self, *, reserve):
1153 +             assert reserve == 16
1154 +             return Buffer()
1155 +
1156 +     statuses = {}
1157 +     sequence = iter(range(1, 100))
1158 +     gl = SimpleNamespace(
1159 +         GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BARRIER_BIT=1,
1160 +         GL_BUFFER_UPDATE_BARRIER_BIT=2,
1161 +         GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER=3,
1162 +         GL_SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE=4,
1163 +         GL_ALREADY_SIGNALED=5,
1164 +         GL_CONDITION_SATISFIED=6,
1165 +         GL_WAIT_FAILED=7,
1166 +         glBindBufferBase=lambda *_: None,
1167 +         glMemoryBarrier=lambda *_: None,
1168 +         glFenceSync=lambda *_: next(sequence),
1169 +         glFlush=lambda: None,
1170 +         glClientWaitSync=lambda sync, *_: statuses[sync],
1171 +         glDeleteSync=lambda *_: None,
1172 +     )
1173 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", SimpleNamespace(GL=gl))
1174 +     ring = DiagnosticRing(Context(), lambda item: item)
1175 +     for group_id in (0, 1):
1176 +         ring.begin(group_id, "output", 0, group_id)
1177 +         ring.end()
1178 +     statuses.update({1: 5, 2: 0})
1179 +     assert [item.group_id for item in ring.poll()] == [0]
1180 +     ring.begin(2, "output", 0, 2)
1181 +     ring.end()
1182 +     statuses.update({3: 5})
1183 +     assert ring.poll() == ()
1184 +     statuses[2] = 5
1185 +     assert [item.group_id for item in ring.poll()] == [1, 2]
1186 +     ring.begin(3, "output", 0, 3)
1187 +     ring.end()
1188 +     statuses[4] = 7
1189 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="fence wait failed"):
1190 +         ring.poll()
1191 +     ring.release()
1192 +
1193 +

1195 +     monkeypatch,
1196 + ):

1203 +         output_id=mapping.output_id,

1206 +         scalar_type="float32",
1207 +         shape=(4, 2),
1208 +     )

1217 +     )

1219 +         update={"uniform_layouts": (clip_layout, arena_layout)}
1220 +     )
1221 +     layer = InstanceSnapshot(
1222 +         layer_setting.instance_id,
1223 +         "texture",
1224 +         True,
1225 +         (
1226 +             ("x", 0.0),
1227 +             ("y", 0.0),
1228 +             ("rotation", 0.0),
1229 +             ("width", 20.0),
1230 +             ("height", 20.0),
1231 +             ("frequency", 0.1),
1232 +             ("phase_x", 0.0),
1233 +             ("phase_y", 0.0),
1234 +             ("opacity", 1.0),
1235 +             ("contrast", 1.0),
1236 +         ),
1237 +         layer_setting,
1238 +     )
1239 +     arena_settings = NS(
1240 +         kind="arena",
1241 +         instance_id="arena",
1242 +         fixed_height_mm=10.0,
1243 +         fixed_pitch_deg=0.0,
1244 +         fixed_roll_deg=0.0,
1245 +         asset_to_world=(
1246 +             (1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1247 +             (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1248 +             (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
1249 +             (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
1250 +         ),
1251 +     )
1252 +     arena = InstanceSnapshot(
1253 +         "arena", "arena", True, (("x", 2.0), ("y", 3.0), ("yaw", 4.0)), arena_settings
1254 +     )
1255 +     scene = NS(arena_instance_id="arena", layer_instance_ids=(layer.instance_id,))
1256 +     events = []
1257 +     renderer = ModernGLSceneRenderer()
1258 +     renderer._moderngl = NS(TRIANGLE_STRIP=5)
1259 +
1260 +     class Uniform:
1261 +         def __init__(self):
1262 +             self.value = None
1263 +             self.bytes = None
1264 +
1265 +         def write(self, value):
1266 +             self.bytes = value
1267 +
1268 +     class Program(dict):
1269 +         def __getitem__(self, key):
1270 +             return self.setdefault(key, Uniform())
1271 +
1272 +     class Quad:
1273 +         def render(self, *, mode):
1274 +             assert mode == renderer._moderngl.TRIANGLE_STRIP
1275 +             events.append("layer")
1276 +
1277 +     frame = NS(quad_array=Quad(), layer_program=Program(), video_textures={})
1278 +
1279 +     def draw_arena(*_args):
1280 +         events.append("arena")
1281 +
1282 +     monkeypatch.setattr(renderer, "_draw_arena", draw_arena)
1283 +     uniforms = {}
1284 +     renderer._draw_scene_layers(
1285 +         frame,
1286 +         artifact,
1287 +         mapping.output_id,
1288 +         mapping,
1289 +         scene,
1290 +         {"arena": arena, layer.instance_id: layer},
1291 +         {},
1292 +         uniforms,
1293 +     )
1294 +     assert events == ["arena", "layer"]
1295 +     assert arena_layout.binding_id in uniforms
1296 +     assert len(uniforms[arena_layout.binding_id].words) == 16
1297 +     assert clip_layout.binding_id in uniforms
1298 +     assert frame.layer_program["clip_vertices"].bytes is not None

1328 +     from pathlib import Path

1341 +         def swap_buffers(self, window):
1342 +             events.append(window)

1355 +     )

1362 + def test_native_window_attachment_preserves_glfw_context_ownership():
1363 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import (
1364 +         NativeRenderingError,
1365 +         attach_window_context,
1366 +     )
1367 +
1368 +     contexts = []
1369 +
1370 +     class GraphicsAPI:
1371 +         def init_context(self):
1372 +             contexts.append(NS(version_code=430, release=lambda: None))
1373 +
1374 +         def get_context(self):
1375 +             return contexts[-1]
1376 +
1377 +     api = GraphicsAPI()
1378 +     first = attach_window_context(api)
1379 +     second = attach_window_context(api)
1380 +     assert first is not second
1381 +     releases = []
1382 +     invalid = NS(version_code=420, release=lambda: releases.append("wrapper"))
1383 +     with pytest.raises(NativeRenderingError, match="OpenGL 4.3"):
1384 +         attach_window_context(
1385 +             NS(init_context=lambda: None, get_context=lambda: invalid)
1386 +         )
1387 +     assert releases == ["wrapper"]
1388 +

1390 + def test_native_failed_gpu_release_keeps_context_for_cleanup_retry():
1391 +     from pathlib import Path
1392 +
1393 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort, _OutputContext
1394 +
1395 +     calls = []
1396 +
1397 +     class Resource:
1398 +         failing = True
1399 +
1400 +         def release(self):
1401 +             if self.failing:
1402 +                 raise RuntimeError("release pending")
1403 +             calls.append("gpu")
1404 +
1405 +     port = ModernGLPort(Path("."), announce=lambda *_: None)
1406 +     port._glfw = NS(
1407 +         make_context_current=lambda _: None,
1408 +         get_current_context=lambda: None,
1409 +         destroy_window=lambda _: calls.append("window"),
1410 +         terminate=lambda: calls.append("glfw"),
1411 +     )
1412 +     resource = Resource()
1413 +     port._resources["prepared:image"] = resource
1414 +     port._outputs["screen"] = _OutputContext(
1415 +         "screen",
1416 +         object(),
1417 +         NS(release=lambda: calls.append("context")),
1418 +         1,
1419 +         1,
1420 +         8,
1421 +         1,
1422 +         None,
1423 +         lambda: None,
1424 +     )
1425 +     port._window_obligations.add("screen")
1426 +     first = port.release()
1427 +     assert first.outstanding and calls == []
1428 +     resource.failing = False
1429 +     second = port.release()
1430 +     assert not second.outstanding
1431 +     assert calls == ["gpu", "context", "window", "glfw"]
1432 +     assert {
1433 +         "visual_stimulus:gpu:prepared:image",
1434 +         "visual_stimulus:window:screen",
1435 +         "visual_stimulus:glfw",
1436 +     } <= set(second.released)
1437 +

1559 + @pytest.mark.parametrize("source,target", [(2.0, 1.0), (1.0, 2.0), (1.0, 1.0)])
1560 + def test_image_fit_uv_spans_preserve_aspect_and_center(source, target):
1561 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.image_fit import fitted_uv_scale
1562 +
1563 +     contain = fitted_uv_scale("contain", source, target)
1564 +     cover = fitted_uv_scale("cover", source, target)
1565 +     assert min(contain) == 1.0 and max(contain) >= 1.0
1566 +     assert max(cover) == 1.0 and min(cover) <= 1.0

1568 +     assert target * cover[1] / cover[0] == pytest.approx(source)
1569 +     assert fitted_uv_scale("stretch", source, target) == (1.0, 1.0)

1626 + @pytest.mark.windows
1627 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1628 +     "fit,face",
1629 +     [("resize", "front"), ("source", "front"), ("fold", "front"), ("fold", "bottom")],
1630 + )
1631 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1632 +     "size,target", [((7, 9), (4, 6)), ((3, 3), (6, 8)), ((7, 3), (4, 6))]
1633 + )
1634 + def test_native_source_shader_preserves_pixels_with_center_crop_and_padding(
1635 +     size, target, fit, face
1636 + ):
1637 +     import moderngl
1638 +     import numpy as np
1639 +
1640 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.image_fit import source_rectangles
1641 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_draw import draw_layer
1642 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.shaders import _LAYER_FRAGMENT, _LAYER_VERTEX
1643 +
1644 +     context = moderngl.create_standalone_context(require=430)
1645 +     try:
1646 +         program = context.program(
1647 +             vertex_shader=_LAYER_VERTEX, fragment_shader=_LAYER_FRAGMENT
1648 +         )
1649 +         vertices = context.buffer(
1650 +             np.array([0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1], dtype="f4").tobytes()
1651 +         )
1652 +         quad = context.vertex_array(program, [(vertices, "2f", "in_uv")])
1653 +         pixels = np.arange(size[0] * size[1] * 4, dtype="f4").reshape(
1654 +             size[1], size[0], 4
1655 +         )
1656 +         pixels /= pixels.size
1657 +         pixels[:, :, 3] = 1
1658 +         texture = context.texture(size, 4, pixels[::-1].tobytes(), dtype="f4")
1659 +         texture.filter = (moderngl.NEAREST, moderngl.NEAREST)
1660 +         texture.use(0)
1661 +         flags = context.buffer(reserve=64)
1662 +         flags.bind_to_storage_buffer(3)
1663 +         target_fbo = context.simple_framebuffer(target, components=4, dtype="f4")
1664 +         target_fbo.use()
1665 +         layouts = tuple(
1666 +             NS(
1667 +                 instance_id="image",
1668 +                 output_id="out",
1669 +                 binding_id=name,
1670 +                 name=f"{name}_map",
1671 +             )
1672 +             for name in (
1673 +                 ("clip_vertices", "image_target_pixels", "image_fold_map")
1674 +                 if fit == "fold"
1675 +                 else ("clip_vertices", "image_target_pixels")
1676 +             )
1677 +         )
1678 +         profile = NS(
1679 +             mapping_id="map",
1680 +             surface_id="front",
1681 +             viewport=NS(x=0, y=0, width=target[0], height=target[1]),
1682 +             valid_area=(0, 0, 1, 1),
1683 +             output_width=target[0],
1684 +             output_height=target[1],
1685 +             columns=2,
1686 +             vertices=tuple(NS(uv=uv, xy=uv) for uv in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1))),
1687 +         )
1688 +         front = NS(**vars(profile))
1689 +         front.mapping_id = "reference"
1690 +         if face == "bottom":
1691 +             profile.surface_id = "bottom"
1692 +             profile.vertices = tuple(
1693 +                 NS(uv=uv, xy=xy)
1694 +                 for uv, xy in zip(
1695 +                     ((0, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1), (1, 0)),
1696 +                     ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1)),
1697 +                     strict=True,
1698 +                 )
1699 +             )
1700 +         artifact = NS(
1701 +             identity=NS(resource_generation="generation"),
1702 +             display=NS(
1703 +                 active_outputs=(NS(output_id="out"),),
1704 +                 geometry=NS(
1705 +                     surfaces=tuple(
1706 +                         NS(
1707 +                             surface_id=face,
1708 +                             bottom_left_mm=(0, 0, 0),
1709 +                             bottom_right_mm=(target[0], 0, 0),
1710 +                             top_left_mm=(0, target[1], 0),
1711 +                         )
1712 +                         for face in ("left", "front", "right", "bottom")
1713 +                     )
1714 +                 ),
1715 +             ),
1716 +             geometric_calibration=(front, profile) if face == "bottom" else (profile,),
1717 +             uniform_layouts=layouts,
1718 +         )
1719 +         snapshot = InstanceSnapshot(
1720 +             "image",
1721 +             "image",
1722 +             True,
1723 +             (("width", 999), ("height", 888)),
1724 +             NS(kind="image", fit=fit, asset_id="asset"),
1725 +         )
1726 +         evidence = draw_layer(
1727 +             moderngl,
1728 +             NS(quad_array=quad, layer_program=program),
1729 +             artifact,
1730 +             {"generation:asset": [texture]},
1731 +             "out",
1732 +             "map",
1733 +             snapshot,
1734 +             [(0, 0)] * 4,
1735 +         )
1736 +         assert {item.binding_id for item in evidence} == {
1737 +             "clip_vertices",
1738 +             "image_target_pixels",
1739 +         } | ({"image_fold_map"} if fit == "fold" else set())
1740 +         assert program["image_target_pixels"].value == (
1741 +             target[::-1] if face == "bottom" else target
1742 +         )
1743 +         actual = np.frombuffer(
1744 +             target_fbo.read(components=4, dtype="f4"), dtype="f4"
1745 +         ).reshape(target[1], target[0], 4)[::-1]
1746 +         expected = np.zeros_like(actual)
1747 +         (sx, sy, w, h), (dx, dy, _, _) = source_rectangles(size, target)
1748 +         expected[dy : dy + h, dx : dx + w] = pixels[sy : sy + h, sx : sx + w]
1749 +         if fit == "resize":
1750 +             xs = ((np.arange(target[0]) + 0.5) * size[0] / target[0]).astype(int)
1751 +             ys = ((np.arange(target[1]) + 0.5) * size[1] / target[1]).astype(int)
1752 +             expected = pixels[ys[:, None], xs]
1753 +         if fit == "fold":
1754 +             expected.fill(0)
1755 +             start_x = target[0] + max(0, -(target[0] + (target[0] - target[1]) / 2))
1756 +             for y in range(target[1]):
1757 +                 for x in range(target[0]):
1758 +                     sx, sy = int(start_x + x + 0.5), y
1759 +                     if face == "bottom":
1760 +                         bx = target[0] + (target[0] - target[1]) / 2
1761 +                         sx, sy = (
1762 +                             int(max(0, bx) + y + 0.5),
1763 +                             target[1] + target[0] - 1 - x,
1764 +                         )
1765 +                     if sx < size[0] and sy < size[1]:
1766 +                         expected[y, x] = pixels[sy, sx]
1767 +         np.testing.assert_array_equal(actual, expected)
1768 +     finally:
1769 +         context.release()
1770 +

1772 + def test_shared_context_wait_uses_unsigned_64_bit_ignored_timeout(monkeypatch):
1773 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.capture_sync import order_shared_outputs
1774 +
1775 +     gl = ModuleType("OpenGL.GL")
1776 +     events = []
1777 +     gl.GL_SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE = 1
1778 +     gl.GL_TIMEOUT_IGNORED = -9223372036854775807
1779 +     gl.glFenceSync = lambda *_args: object()
1780 +     gl.glFlush = lambda: None
1781 +     gl.glWaitSync = lambda fence, flags, timeout: events.append((flags, timeout))
1782 +     gl.glDeleteSync = lambda fence: None
1783 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL.GL", gl)
1784 +     root = ModuleType("OpenGL")
1785 +     root.GL = gl
1786 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", root)
1787 +     pending = []
1788 +     order_shared_outputs(
1789 +         {"one": NS(activate=lambda: None), "two": NS(activate=lambda: None)}, pending
1790 +     )
1791 +     assert events == [(0, 18446744073709551615)] * 2
1792 +     assert not pending

1795 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1796 +     ("pixel_format", "dtype"),
1797 +     [("rgba8_top_down", "f1"), ("r10g10b10a2_le_top_down", "f4")],
1798 + )
1799 + def test_review_capture_clears_framebuffer_in_creation_order(
1800 +     monkeypatch, pixel_format, dtype
1801 + ):
1802 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.capture import ReviewCapture
1803 +
1804 +     gl = ModuleType("OpenGL.GL")
1805 +     gl.GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER = 1
1806 +     gl.glBindBuffer = lambda *_args: None
1807 +
1808 +     def stop(*_args):
1809 +         raise RuntimeError("reached PBO allocation")
1810 +
1811 +     gl.glGenBuffers = stop
1812 +     root = ModuleType("OpenGL")
1813 +     root.GL = gl
1814 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", root)
1815 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL.GL", gl)
1816 +     modern = ModuleType("moderngl")
1817 +     modern.TRIANGLE_STRIP = 1
1818 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "moderngl", modern)
1819 +     events = []
1820 +     texture = NS()
1821 +     fbo = NS(
1822 +         use=lambda: events.append("use"), clear=lambda *_args: events.append("clear")
1823 +     )
1824 +     texture_types = []
1825 +
1826 +     def allocate_texture(*_args, **kwargs):
1827 +         texture_types.append(kwargs["dtype"])
1828 +         return texture
1829 +
1830 +     context = NS(
1831 +         texture=allocate_texture,
1832 +         framebuffer=lambda **_kwargs: fbo,
1833 +         program=lambda **_kwargs: {"source_tex": NS(value=None)},
1834 +         buffer=lambda *_args: NS(),
1835 +         vertex_array=lambda *_args, **_kwargs: NS(),
1836 +     )
1837 +     capture = ReviewCapture()
1838 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="reached PBO allocation"):
1839 +         capture.capture(
1840 +             (NS(output_id="one", texture_handle=NS(ctx=context)),),
1841 +             NS(
1842 +                 composite_width=2,
1843 +                 composite_height=2,
1844 +                 tiles=(),
1845 +                 native_pixel_format=pixel_format,
1846 +             ),
1847 +         )
1848 +     assert events == ["use", "clear", "use"]
1849 +     assert texture_types == [dtype, dtype]
1850 +     capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, pixel_format)
1851 +     assert texture_types == [dtype, dtype]
1852 +     other = (
1853 +         "r10g10b10a2_le_top_down"
1854 +         if pixel_format == "rgba8_top_down"
1855 +         else "rgba8_top_down"
1856 +     )
1857 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="cannot change"):
1858 +         capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, other)
1859 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unsupported"):
1860 +         capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, "unknown")
1861 +

1863 + @pytest.mark.parametrize("mapping", ["valid", "missing", "corrupted"])
1864 + def test_review_capture_polls_fence_then_fills_owned_host_buffer(mapping):
1865 +     import ctypes
1866 +
1867 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.capture import (
1868 +         ReviewCapture,
1869 +         _Buffer,
1870 +         _Pending,
1871 +     )
1872 +
1873 +     events = []
1874 +     signaled = False
1875 +
1876 +     expected = b"\x00\x01\x7f\xff\x02\x03\x80\xfe"
1877 +     storage = (ctypes.c_ubyte * 8).from_buffer_copy(expected)
1878 +
1879 +     def read(target, offset, size, flags):
1880 +         assert (target, offset, size, flags) == (1, 0, 8, 16)
1881 +         events.append("map")
1882 +         return None if mapping == "missing" else ctypes.addressof(storage)
1883 +
1884 +     def unmap(target):
1885 +         assert target == 1
1886 +         events.append("unmap")
1887 +         storage[:] = bytes(8)
1888 +         return mapping != "corrupted"
1889 +
1890 +     gl = NS(
1891 +         GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER=1,
1892 +         GL_ALREADY_SIGNALED=2,
1893 +         GL_CONDITION_SATISFIED=3,
1894 +         GL_WAIT_FAILED=4,
1895 +         GL_MAP_READ_BIT=16,
1896 +         glClientWaitSync=lambda *_args: 2 if signaled else 0,
1897 +         glBindBuffer=lambda *_args: None,
1898 +         glMapBufferRange=read,
1899 +         glUnmapBuffer=unmap,
1900 +         glDeleteSync=lambda *_args: events.append("fence"),
1901 +         glDeleteBuffers=lambda *_args: events.append("buffer"),
1902 +     )
1903 +     capture = ReviewCapture()
1904 +     pending = _Pending(_Buffer(gl, 7, 8), 8)
1905 +     capture._pending.append(pending)
1906 +     assert capture.poll(pending) is None
1907 +     assert not events
1908 +     signaled = True
1909 +     if mapping != "valid":
1910 +         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="mapping"):
1911 +             capture.poll(pending)
1912 +         assert capture._pending == [pending]
1913 +         with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="cleanup is required"):
1914 +             capture.poll(pending)
1915 +         capture.release()
1916 +         assert not capture._pending
1917 +         assert events[-2:] == ["fence", "buffer"]
1918 +         return
1919 +     pixels = capture.poll(pending)
1920 +     assert isinstance(pixels, bytearray)
1921 +     assert pixels == expected
1922 +     assert events == ["map", "unmap", "fence"]
1923 +     assert capture._available == [pending.buffer]
1924 +     assert not capture._pending
1925 +     with pytest.raises(TypeError, match="invalid pending"):
1926 +         capture.poll(pending)
1927 +
1928 +     capture.release()
1929 +     assert not capture._available
1930 +     assert events[-1] == "buffer"
1931 +

1933 + def test_context_activation_checks_actual_pointer_before_skipping_rebind():
1934 +     import ctypes
1935 +
1936 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import activate_window
1937 +
1938 +     target = ctypes.pointer(ctypes.c_int(1))
1939 +     other = ctypes.pointer(ctypes.c_int(2))
1940 +     current = ctypes.cast(target, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_int))
1941 +     bindings = []
1942 +     glfw = NS(
1943 +         get_current_context=lambda: current,
1944 +         make_context_current=bindings.append,
1945 +     )
1946 +     activate_window(glfw, target)
1947 +     assert not bindings
1948 +     current = other
1949 +     activate_window(glfw, target)
1950 +     assert bindings == [target]
1951 +     current = None
1952 +     activate_window(glfw, target)
1953 +     assert bindings == [target, target]

1956 + def test_legacy_texture_recipe_parser_bounds_and_normalizes_json():
1957 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import parse_texture_recipe
1958 +
1959 +     recipe = parse_texture_recipe(
1960 +         b'{"cephvr_asset_type":"texture_design","kind":"texture",'
1961 +         b'"pattern":"sine_grating","params":{"size":64,"tile_width_mm":88}}'
1962 +     )
1963 +     assert (recipe.pattern, recipe.resolution, recipe.tile_width_mm) == (
1964 +         "grating",
1965 +         64,
1966 +         88,
1967 +     )
1968 +     resolved = parse_texture_recipe(
1969 +         b'{"pattern":"flat","params":{"size":32,"texture_resolution":48}}'
1970 +     )
1971 +     assert resolved.resolution == 48
1972 +     assert (
1973 +         parse_texture_recipe(
1974 +             b'{"pattern":"flat","params":{"texture_resolution":null}}'
1975 +         ).resolution
1976 +         == 512
1977 +     )
1978 +     assert (
1979 +         parse_texture_recipe(
1980 +             b'{"pattern":"flat","params":{"texture_resolution":""}}'
1981 +         ).resolution
1982 +         == 512
1983 +     )
1984 +     legacy = parse_texture_recipe(
1985 +         b'{"pattern":"vertical_bars","pattern_params":{"texture_resolution":32}}'
1986 +     )
1987 +     assert (legacy.pattern, legacy.resolution) == ("bars_vertical", 32)
1988 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="Unsupported legacy texture pattern"):
1989 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"future_pattern"}')
1990 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="positive integer"):
1991 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"flat","params":{"size":0}}')
1992 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="finite"):
1993 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"grating","params":{"phase_cycles":NaN}}')
1994 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="allocation limit"):
1995 +         parse_texture_recipe(
1996 +             b'{"pattern":"flat","params":{"size":65}}', max_texture_pixels=4096
1997 +         )
1998 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document limit"):
1999 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"flat"}', max_document_bytes=4)
2000 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="document limit"):
2001 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"flat"}', max_document_bytes=0)
2002 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="kind must be 'texture'"):
2003 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"kind":"arena","pattern":"checker"}')
2004 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="kind must be 'texture'"):
2005 +         parse_texture_recipe(
2006 +             b'{"cephvr_asset_type":"texture_design","kind":"arena","pattern":"checker"}'
2007 +         )
2008 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="does not contain legacy texture"):
2009 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"name":"a program document"}')
2010 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="duplicate JSON key"):
2011 +         parse_texture_recipe(b'{"pattern":"flat","pattern":"checker"}')
2012 +     deeply_nested = (
2013 +         b'{"pattern":"flat","params":' + b'{"x":' * 65 + b"0" + b"}" * 65 + b"}"
2014 +     )
2015 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="nesting budget"):
2016 +         parse_texture_recipe(deeply_nested)
2017 +

2178 + def test_legacy_polar_texture_requires_complete_geometry_and_matches_reference():
2179 +     import hashlib
2180 +     import json
2181 +
2182 +     import numpy as np
2183 +
2184 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import (
2185 +         TextureRecipe,
2186 +         generate_texture_recipe,
2187 +         parse_texture_recipe,
2188 +     )
2189 +
2190 +     corners = {
2191 +         "front": np.array(
2192 +             [[-50, 0, 0], [50, 0, 0], [50, 0, 80], [-50, 0, 80]], dtype=np.float32
2193 +         ),
2194 +         "left": np.array(
2195 +             [[-50, -100, 0], [-50, 0, 0], [-50, 0, 80], [-50, -100, 80]],
2196 +             dtype=np.float32,
2197 +         ),
2198 +         "right": np.array(
2199 +             [[50, 0, 0], [50, 100, 0], [50, 100, 80], [50, 0, 80]], dtype=np.float32
2200 +         ),
2201 +         "bottom": np.array(
2202 +             [[-50, -100, 0], [50, -100, 0], [50, 0, 0], [-50, 0, 0]], dtype=np.float32
2203 +         ),
2204 +     }
2205 +     recipe = TextureRecipe(
2206 +         "polar_grating",
2207 +         {
2208 +             "origin_screen": "front",
2209 +             "target_screen": "right",
2210 +             "bar_count": 8,
2211 +             "phase_deg": 12,
2212 +             "bar_width_mm": 10,
2213 +             "feature_sharpness": 0.7,
2214 +             "inner_radius_mm": 3,
2215 +             "outer_radius_mm": 200,
2216 +         },
2217 +         64,
2218 +         None,
2219 +         None,
2220 +     )
2221 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="surface corners"):
2222 +         generate_texture_recipe(recipe)
2223 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="corners for bottom"):
2224 +         generate_texture_recipe(
2225 +             recipe,
2226 +             surface_corners={
2227 +                 key: value for key, value in corners.items() if key != "bottom"
2228 +             },
2229 +         )
2230 +     pixels = generate_texture_recipe(recipe, surface_corners=corners)
2231 +     assert (
2232 +         hashlib.sha256(pixels.tobytes()).hexdigest()
2233 +         == "bfb631bdbde155bc01cea1a4d81cde8fea509aac0eb7128d5bc57bc84fab4ab3"
2234 +     )
2235 +     for pattern in ("grating", "polar_grating"):
2236 +         for channel in ("color_a", "color_b"):
2237 +             for values, clamped in (
2238 +                 ([1e308, -1e308, 0.5], [1, 0, 0.5]),
2239 +                 ([-1e308, 0.5, 1e308], [0, 0.5, 1]),
2240 +             ):
2241 +                 rasters = [
2242 +                     generate_texture_recipe(
2243 +                         parse_texture_recipe(
2244 +                             json.dumps(
2245 +                                 {
2246 +                                     "pattern": pattern,
2247 +                                     "params": {"size": 8, channel: rgb},
2248 +                                 }
2249 +                             ).encode()
2250 +                         ),
2251 +                         surface_corners=corners,
2252 +                     )
2253 +                     for rgb in (values, clamped)
2254 +                 ]
2255 +                 np.testing.assert_array_equal(*rasters)

2258 + def test_legacy_image_recipe_requires_explicit_decoded_rgb_pixels():
2259 +     import numpy as np
2260 +
2261 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import (
2262 +         TextureRecipe,
2263 +         generate_texture_recipe,
2264 +     )
2265 +
2266 +     recipe = TextureRecipe("image", {"texture_path": "ignored.png"}, 4, None, None)
2267 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="explicitly decoded"):
2268 +         generate_texture_recipe(recipe)
2269 +     source = np.arange(24, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(2, 4, 3)
2270 +     assert np.array_equal(generate_texture_recipe(recipe, image_pixels=source), source)
2271 +     gray = np.arange(8, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(2, 4)
2272 +     rgb_gray = generate_texture_recipe(recipe, image_pixels=gray)
2273 +     assert rgb_gray.shape == (2, 4, 3)
2274 +     assert np.array_equal(rgb_gray[:, :, 0], gray)
2275 +     assert np.array_equal(rgb_gray[:, :, 1], gray)
2276 +     rgba = np.concatenate((source, np.full((2, 4, 1), 128, dtype=np.uint8)), axis=2)
2277 +     assert np.array_equal(generate_texture_recipe(recipe, image_pixels=rgba), source)
2278 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="8-bit"):
2279 +         generate_texture_recipe(recipe, image_pixels=source.astype(np.uint16))

2321 + def test_legacy_texture_generator_checks_deadline_during_dot_loops():
2322 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import (
2323 +         TextureRecipe,
2324 +         generate_texture_recipe,
2325 +     )
2326 +
2327 +     recipe = TextureRecipe(
2328 +         "dots_rand",
2329 +         {"density_per_cm2": 3, "tile_size_mm": 100, "seed": 1},
2330 +         16,
2331 +         None,
2332 +         None,
2333 +     )
2334 +     checks = 0
2335 +
2336 +     def expire_after_three_checks():
2337 +         nonlocal checks
2338 +         checks += 1
2339 +         if checks == 3:
2340 +             raise TimeoutError("preparation deadline expired")
2341 +
2342 +     with pytest.raises(TimeoutError, match="deadline expired"):
2343 +         generate_texture_recipe(recipe, check=expire_after_three_checks)
2344 +     assert checks == 3

2390 + def test_texture_recipe_numeric_boolean_values_match_boolean_flags() -> None:
2391 +     import json
2392 +
2393 +     import numpy as np
2394 +
2395 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import (
2396 +         generate_texture_recipe,
2397 +         parse_texture_recipe,
2398 +     )
2399 +
2400 +     base = {
2401 +         "pattern": "grating",
2402 +         "params": {"texture_resolution": 8, "cycles_per_texture": 2.5},
2403 +     }
2404 +     for numeric, flag in ((0.0, False), (1.0, True)):
2405 +         base["params"]["seamless"] = numeric
2406 +         numeric_pixels = generate_texture_recipe(
2407 +             parse_texture_recipe(json.dumps(base).encode())
2408 +         )
2409 +         base["params"]["seamless"] = flag
2410 +         boolean_pixels = generate_texture_recipe(
2411 +             parse_texture_recipe(json.dumps(base).encode())
2412 +         )
2413 +         np.testing.assert_array_equal(numeric_pixels, boolean_pixels)

2415 +
2416 + def _resident_images(*, count=8, batch=False, progress=None):
2417 +     import threading
2418 +
2419 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.image_residency import ImageResidency
2420 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.budget import BoundedBudget
2421 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.image_schedule import ImageSchedule
2422 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.image_source import ImageSource
2423 +
2424 +     gate = threading.Event()
2425 +     loads = []
2426 +     sources = {}
2427 +     for index in range(count):
2428 +         key = f"image-{index}"
2429 +
2430 +         def load(check, index=index):
2431 +             loads.append((index, threading.get_ident()))
2432 +             if index == 3:
2433 +                 while not gate.wait(0.001):
2434 +                     check()
2435 +             check()
2436 +             return bytes([index]) * 16
2437 +
2438 +         sources[key] = ImageSource(key, 1, 1, 192, 16, load)
2439 +     schedule = ImageSchedule(
2440 +         sources,
2441 +         {("trial", key): key for key in sources},
2442 +         {"trial": "generation"},
2443 +         {"trial": tuple((("layer", key),) for key in sources)},
2444 +         {("trial", "layer"): "image-0"},
2445 +         {("trial", key): index for index, key in enumerate(sources)},
2446 +     )
2447 +     budget = BoundedBudget(cpu_limit=192, gpu_limit=48)
2448 +     registrations = []
2449 +     sealed = False
2450 +
2451 +     def register_many(items):
2452 +         if sealed:
2453 +             raise RuntimeError("cannot add resources after Ready")
2454 +         registrations.append(items)
2455 +
2456 +     cache = ImageResidency(
2457 +         schedule,
2458 +         budget,
2459 +         announce=lambda *args: None,
2460 +         announce_batch=register_many if batch else None,
2461 +         progress=progress,
2462 +     )
2463 +
2464 +     class GPU:
2465 +         release_allowed = True
2466 +         fail_release = False
2467 +         uploads = []
2468 +
2469 +         def upload(self, source, pixels, textures):
2470 +             self.uploads.append((source.key, pixels))
2471 +             textures.append(source.key)
2472 +             return True
2473 +
2474 +         def fence(self, textures, fences):
2475 +             fences.append(True)
2476 +
2477 +         def ready(self, fences):
2478 +             if not self.release_allowed:
2479 +                 return False
2480 +             fences.clear()
2481 +             return True
2482 +
2483 +         def release(self, textures, fences):
2484 +             if self.fail_release:
2485 +                 raise RuntimeError("release rejected")
2486 +             textures.clear()
2487 +             fences.clear()
2488 +
2489 +     gpu = GPU()
2490 +     aliases = {}
2491 +     cache.attach(gpu, aliases, lambda key, path: register_many(((key, path),)))
2492 +     assert len(registrations) == (1 if batch else count)
2493 +     assert sum(len(items) for items in registrations) == count
2494 +     assert budget.usage.gpu_bytes == 0
2495 +     sealed = True
2496 +     return cache, budget, gpu, gate, loads, aliases
2497 +
2498 +
2499 + def _resident_snapshot(asset, position=0):
2500 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.program_model import ImageTile
2501 +
2502 +     setting = make_prepared_trial().epochs[0].settings[0]
2503 +     setting = setting.model_copy(
2504 +         update={
2505 +             "instance_id": "layer",
2506 +             "pattern": ImageTile(
2507 +                 kind="image_tile",
2508 +                 asset_id=asset,
2509 +                 period_x={"kind": "constant", "value": 10},
2510 +                 period_y={"kind": "constant", "value": 10},
2511 +                 sampling="linear",
2512 +             ),
2513 +         }
2514 +     )
2515 +     return InstanceSnapshot(
2516 +         "layer", "texture", True, (("texture_phase_x", position),), setting
2517 +     )
2518 +

2520 + @pytest.mark.parametrize("batch", [False, True])
2521 + def test_image_residency_holds_actual_texture_with_current_motion_and_bounded_reload(
2522 +     batch,
2523 + ):
2524 +     import threading
2525 +     import time
2526 +
2527 +     cache, budget, gpu, gate, loads, aliases = _resident_images(batch=batch)
2528 +     try:
2529 +         cache.prepare(time.perf_counter_ns() + 2_000_000_000)
2530 +         assert set(index for index, _ in loads) == {0, 1, 2}
2531 +         assert all(thread != threading.get_ident() for _, thread in loads)
2532 +         first = cache.select("trial", 0, (_resident_snapshot("image-0"),))[0]
2533 +         assert first.image_selection.image_index == 0
2534 +         assert not first.image_selection.repeated
2535 +         deadline = time.monotonic() + 2
2536 +         held = cache.select("trial", 3, (_resident_snapshot("image-3", 75),))[0]
2537 +         assert held.settings.pattern.asset_id == "image-0"
2538 +         assert held.state == (("texture_phase_x", 75),)
2539 +         assert held.image_selection.requested_image_index == 3
2540 +         assert held.image_selection.disposition == "residency_hold"
2541 +         assert held.image_selection.repeated
2542 +         while not any(index == 3 for index, _ in loads) and time.monotonic() < deadline:
2543 +             cache.select("trial", 3, (_resident_snapshot("image-3", 80),))
2544 +             time.sleep(0.001)
2545 +         assert any(index == 3 for index, _ in loads)
2546 +         assert budget.usage.cpu_bytes == 192
2547 +         assert budget.usage.gpu_bytes <= 48
2548 +         gate.set()
2549 +         while time.monotonic() < deadline:
2550 +             shown = cache.select("trial", 3, (_resident_snapshot("image-3", 90),))[0]
2551 +             if shown.image_selection.image_index == 3:
2552 +                 break
2553 +             time.sleep(0.001)
2554 +         assert shown.settings.pattern.asset_id == "image-3"
2555 +         assert shown.state == (("texture_phase_x", 90),)
2556 +         assert not shown.image_selection.repeated
2557 +         assert shown.image_selection.disposition == "current"
2558 +         assert aliases["generation:image-3"] == ["image-3"]
2559 +         repeated = cache.select("trial", 3, (_resident_snapshot("image-3", 100),))[0]
2560 +         assert repeated.image_selection.repeated
2561 +         assert budget.usage.cpu_bytes <= 192
2562 +         assert budget.usage.gpu_bytes <= 48
2563 +         cache.finish("trial")
2564 +         cache.begin("trial")
2565 +         restarted = cache.select("trial", 0, (_resident_snapshot("image-0"),))[0]
2566 +         assert restarted.image_selection.image_index == 0
2567 +         assert not restarted.image_selection.repeated
2568 +     finally:
2569 +         gate.set()
2570 +         assert cache.close(time.perf_counter_ns() + 2_000_000_000)
2571 +         assert cache.release_gpu() == ()
2572 +     assert budget.usage.cpu_bytes == budget.usage.gpu_bytes == 0
2573 +     assert not aliases

2577 +     import time
2578 +

2600 +         cache.close(time.perf_counter_ns() + 2_000_000_000)
